=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def stoneGameVI(self, a: List[int], b: List[int]) -> int:
        combines = [(a[i] + c[i], a[i], b[i]) for i in range(len(a))]
        combines.sort(reverse=True)
        bobPoints = sum(b)
        alicePoints = 0
        for i in range(0, len(a), 2):
            alicePoints += combines[i][1]
            bobPoints -= combines[i][2]
        if alicePoints > bobPoints:
            return 1
        elif alicePoints < bobPoints:
            return -1
        return 0

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def stoneGameVI(self, a: List[int], b: List[int]) -> int:
        combines = [(a[i] + b[i], a[i], b[i]) for i in range(len(a))]
        combines.sort(reverse=True)
        bobPoints = sum(b)
        alicePoints = 0
        for i in range(0, len(a), 2):
            alicePoints += combines[i][1]
            bobPoints -= combines[i][2]
        if alicePoints > bobPoints:
            return 1
        elif alicePoints < bobPoints:
            return -1
        return 0

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def stoneGameVI(self, a: List[int], b: List[int]) -> int:
        combines = [(a[i] + b[i], a[i], b[i]) for i in range(len(a))]
        combines.sort(reverse=True)
        bobPoints = sum(b)
        alicePoints = 0
        for i in range(0, len(a), 2):
            alicePoints += combines[i][1]
            bobPoints -= combines[i][2]
        if alicePoints > bobPoints:
            return 1
        elif alicePoints < bobPoints:
            return -1
        return 0

Let's maybe recall official solution code:
```
class Solution:
    def stoneGameVI(self, aliceValues: List[int], bobValues: List[int]) -> int:
        values = [[a+b, a, b] for a,b in zip(aliceValues,bobValues)]
        values.sort(reverse=True)
        alice = sum(a for _,a,_ in values[::2])
        bob = sum(b for _,_,b in values[1::2])
        return (alice > bob) - (alice < bob)
```
Actually official uses values.sort(reverse=True) with list [a+b,a,b], same. So our code equivalent. Good.

=== RAW ANSWER ===
. Need output only fixed code. I can include code block? The final answer should be code only. I'll output plain code with import. Ensure no explanation.</think>```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def stoneGameVI(self, a: List[int], b: List[int]) -> int:
        combines = [(a[i] + b[i], a[i], b[i]) for i in range(len(a))]
        combines.sort(reverse=True)

        bobPoints = sum(b)
        alicePoints = 0

        for i in range(0, len(a), 2):
            alicePoints += combines[i][1]
            bobPoints -= combines[i][2]

        if alicePoints > bobPoints:
            return 1
        elif alicePoints < bobPoints:
            return -1
        return 0
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def stoneGameVI(self, a: List[int], b: List[int]) -> int:
        combines = [(a[i] + b[i], a[i], b[i]) for i in range(len(a))]
        combines.sort(reverse=True)

        bobPoints = sum(b)
        alicePoints = 0

        for i in range(0, len(a), 2):
            alicePoints += combines[i][1]
            bobPoints -= combines[i][2]

        if alicePoints > bobPoints:
            return 1
        elif alicePoints < bobPoints:
            return -1
        return 0
